Spec: "invalid values (zero, negatives, floats, strings) log a warning via npmlog with bail_on_test_failure as prefix and default to false."
`log.warn('bail_on_test_failure', msg)` gives prefix `bail_on_test_failure`. Good.

Note: `config.get('bail_on_test_failure')` returns false by default (from defaults). For false: `Number.isInteger(false)` false => logs warning! Wait,
false is an invalid value? The default is false, and false should NOT log a warning. So we must handle false specially: if `false`, set
`bailOnFailures = false` without warning. The validation should treat `false` as valid. Let me write:

```js
this.bailOnFailures = config.get('bail_on_test_failure');
if (this.bailOnFailures === true) {
  this.bailOnFailures = 1;

  log.warn(...);
  this.bailOnFailures = false;

```

Also `null` and `undefined`? config default is false, so config.get returns false. But if user sets null... Treat null/undefined as invalid? The spec
lists "zero, negatives, floats, strings" as invalid. false is valid (default). true maps to 1. I'll treat anything other than false and positive
integer as invalid (log warning). So:

```js
this.bailOnFailures = config.get('bail_on_test_failure');
if (this.bailOnFailures === true) {
  this.bailOnFailures = 1;
}

if (this.bailOnFailures !== false && (!Number.isInteger(this.bailOnFailures) || this.bailOnFailures < 1)) {
  log.warn('bail_on_test_failure', `Invalid value for bail_on_test_failure: ${this.bailOnFailures}`);
  this.bailOnFailures = false;

```

That covers zero (Number.isInteger(0) true but <1 => invalid), negatives, floats, strings.

summary += '\n# bailed\n# ran before bail ' + report.testsRanBeforeBail + '\n# suppressed ' + this.app.reporter.suppressed;

'use strict';

const Bluebird = require('bluebird');
const log = require('npmlog');
const EventEmitter = require('events').EventEmitter;

const reporters = require('../reporters');
const isa = require('./isa');
const ReportFile = require('./report-file');

function setupReporter(name, out, config, app) {
  let reporter;

  if (isa(name, String)) {
    let TestReporter = reporters[name];
    if (TestReporter) {
      reporter = new TestReporter(false, out, config, app);
    }
  } else if (isa(name, Function)) {
    // name is a constructor function, ignore new-cap and instantiate
    // eslint-disable-next-line new-cap
    reporter = new name(false, out, config, app);
  } else {
    reporter = name;
  }

  if (!reporter) {
    throw new Error('Test reporter `' + name + '` not found.');
  }

  return reporter;
}


class Reporter extends EventEmitter {
  constructor(app, stdout, path) {
    super();
    this.total = 0;
    this.passed = 0;
    this.skipped = 0;
    this.todo = 0;

    if (path) {
      this.reportFile = new ReportFile(path);
    }

    let config = app.config;

    if (path && config.get('xunit_intermediate_output') && config.get('reporter') === 'xunit') {
      this.reporters = [
        setupReporter('tap', stdout, config, app),
        setupReporter(config.get('reporter'), this.reportFile.outputStream, config, app)
      ];
    } else {
      this.reporters = [setupReporter(config.get('reporter'), stdout, config, app)];

      if (path) {
        if (config.appMode === 'dev') {
          let devModeFileReporter = config.get('dev_mode_file_reporter');
          if (!devModeFileReporter) {
            log.warn('You configured a `report_file`, you may want to configure the `dev_mode_file_reporter` as well. Using the `tap` logger now.');
            devModeFileReporter = 'tap';
          }
          this.reporters.push(setupReporter(devModeFileReporter, this.reportFile.outputStream, config, app));
        } else {
          this.reporters.push(setupReporter(config.get('reporter'), this.reportFile.outputStream, config, app));
        }
      }
    }

    this.bailOnFailures = config.get('bail_on_test_failure');
    if (this.bailOnFailures === true) {
      this.bailOnFailures = 1;
    }

    if (this.bailOnFailures !== false && (!Number.isInteger(this.bailOnFailures) || this.bailOnFailures < 1)) {
      log.warn('bail_on_test_failure', `Invalid bail_on_test_failure value: ${this.bailOnFailures}. Defaulting to false.`);
      this.bailOnFailures = false;
    }

    this.bailReason = null;
    this.bailLauncher = null;
    this.testsRanBeforeBail = 0;
    this.failuresByLauncher = {};
    this.failedTests = [];
    this.suppressed = 0;
  }

  testStarted(name, data) {
    this.reporters.forEach(reporter => {
      if (reporter.testStarted) {
        reporter.testStarted(name, data);
      }
    });
  }

  close() {
    this.finish();

    if (this.reportFile) {
      return this.reportFile.close();
    }
  }

  hasTests() {
    return this.total > 0;
  }

  hasPassed() {
    return this.total <= ((this.passed || 0) + (this.skipped || 0) + (this.todo || 0));
  }

  hasBailed() {
    return !!this.bailReason;
  }

  getBailReport() {
    return {
      testsRanBeforeBail: this.testsRanBeforeBail,
      bailLauncher: this.bailLauncher,
      failuresByLauncher: this.failuresByLauncher,
      failedTests: this.failedTests
    };
  }

  resetBailState() {
    this.bailReason = null;
    this.bailLauncher = null;
    this.testsRanBeforeBail = 0;
    this.failuresByLauncher = {};
    this.failedTests = [];
    this.suppressed = 0;
  }

  report(name, result) {
    this.total++;
    if (result.skipped) {
      this.skipped++;
    } else if (result.passed && !result.todo) {
      this.passed++;
    } else if (!result.passed && result.todo) {
      this.todo++;
    }

    if (this.hasBailed()) {
      this.suppressed++;
      return;
    }

    this.reporters.forEach(reporter => {
      reporter.report(name, result);
    });

    if (!result.passed && !result.skipped && !result.todo) {
      this.failuresByLauncher[name] = (this.failuresByLauncher[name] || 0) + 1;
      this.failedTests.push(result.name);
      if (this.bailOnFailures && this.failuresByLauncher[name] >= this.bailOnFailures) {
        this.bailReason = result.name;
        this.bailLauncher = name;
        this.testsRanBeforeBail = this.total - 1;
        this.emit('test-failure', name, result);
        this.reporters.forEach(reporter => {
          if (reporter.bailOut) {
            reporter.bailOut(name, result);
          }
        });
      }
    }
  }
}

Reporter.with = (app, stdout, path) => Bluebird.try(() => new Reporter(app, stdout, path)).disposer((reporter, promise) => {
  if (promise.isRejected()) {
    let err = promise.reason();

    if (!err.hideFromReporter) {
      reporter.report(null, {
        passed: false,
        name: err.name || 'unknown error',
        error: {
          message: err.message
        }
      });
    }
  }

  return reporter.close();
});

function forwardToReporters(fn) {
  return function() {
    let args = new Array(arguments.length);
    for (let i = 0; i < args.length; ++i) {
      args[i] = arguments[i];
    }

    this.reporters.forEach(reporter => {
      if (reporter[fn]) {
        reporter[fn].apply(reporter, args);
      }
    });
  };
}

['finish', 'onStart', 'onEnd', 'reportMetadata'].forEach(fn => {
  Reporter.prototype[fn] = forwardToReporters(fn);
});

module.exports = Reporter;
EOF

'use strict';

const displayutils = require('../utils/displayutils');

module.exports = class TapReporter {
  constructor(silent, out, config, app) {
    this.out = out || process.stdout;
    this.silent = silent;
    this.quietLogs = !!config.get('tap_quiet_logs');
    this.failsOnly = !!config.get('tap_failed_tests_only');
    this.strictSpecCompliance = !!config.get('tap_strict_spec_compliance');
    this.stoppedOnError = null;
    this.id = 1;
    this.total = 0;
    this.pass = 0;
    this.skipped = 0;
    this.todo = 0;
    this.results = [];
    this.errors = [];
    this.logs = [];
    this.logProcessor = config.get('tap_log_processor');
    this.app = app;
  }

  report(prefix, data) {
    if (this.hasBailed()) {
      return;
    }
    this.results.push({
      launcher: prefix,
      result: data
    });
    this.display(prefix, data);
    this.total++;

    if (data.skipped) {
      this.skipped++;
    } else if (data.passed && !data.todo) {
      this.pass++;
    } else if (!data.passed && data.todo) {
      this.todo++;
    }
  }

  summaryDisplay() {
    let summary = displayutils.summaryDisplay.call(this);
    if (this.hasBailed()) {
      let report = this.getBailReport();
      summary += `\n# bailed\n# ran before bail ${report.testsRanBeforeBail}\n# suppressed ${this.getSuppressed()}`;
    }
    return summary;
  }

  bailOut() {
    if (this.silent) {
      return;
    }
    let report = this.getBailReport();
    let count = report.failuresByLauncher[report.bailLauncher] || 0;
    this.out.write(`Bail out! ${report.bailReason} (${count})\n`);
  }

  hasBailed() {
    return !!(this.app && this.app.reporter && this.app.reporter.hasBailed());
  }

  getBailReport() {
    return this.app.reporter.getBailReport();
  }

  getSuppressed() {
    return this.app.reporter.suppressed;
  }

  /*
   * Based on current settings in this object, will the given value be
   * displayed by 'display'?
   */
  willDisplay(result) {
    let show = !this.silent && !!result && (!this.failsOnly || result.error);
    return show;
  }

  /*
   * Display a formatted message for the result, but only if
   * we've configured to do that.
   */
  display(prefix, result) {
    if (this.willDisplay(result)) {
      this.out.write(displayutils.resultString(this.id++, prefix, result, this.quietLogs, this.strictSpecCompliance, this.logProcessor));
    }
  }

  finish() {
    if (this.silent) {
      return;
    }
    this.out.write('\n' + this.summaryDisplay() + '\n');
  }
};
EOF

cd /app && cat > lib/reporters/dot_reporter.js <<'EOF'
'use strict';

const indent = require('../utils/strutils').indent;
const printf = require('printf');
const displayutils = require('../utils/displayutils');

module.exports = class DotReporter {
  constructor(silent, out, config, app) {
    this.out = out || process.stdout;
    this.silent = silent;
    this.stoppedOnError = null;
    this.id = 1;
    this.total = 0;
    this.pass = 0;
    this.skipped = 0;
    this.todo = 0;
    this.results = [];
    this.startTime = new Date();
    this.endTime = null;
    this.currentLineChars = 0;
    this.maxLineChars = Math.min(this.out.columns || 65, 65) - 5;
    this.app = app;
    this.out.write('\n');
    this.out.write('  ');
  }

  report(prefix, data) {
    if (this.hasBailed()) {
      return;
    }
    this.results.push({
      launcher: prefix,
      result: data
    });
    this.display(prefix, data);
    this.total++;
    if (data.skipped) {
      this.skipped++;
    } else if (data.passed && !data.todo) {
      this.pass++;
    } else if (!data.passed && data.todo) {
      this.todo++;
    }
  }

  bailOut() {
    if (this.silent) {
      return;
    }
    let report = this.getBailReport();
    let count = report.failuresByLauncher[report.bailLauncher] || 0;
    this.out.write(`\nBail out! ${report.bailReason} (${count})\n`);
  }

  hasBailed() {
    return !!(this.app && this.app.reporter && this.app.reporter.hasBailed());
  }

  getBailReport() {
    return this.app.reporter.getBailReport();
  }

  getSuppressed() {
    return this.app.reporter.suppressed;
  }

  display(prefix, result) {
    if (this.silent) {
      return;
    }
    if (this.currentLineChars > this.maxLineChars) {
      this.currentLineChars = 0;
      this.out.write('\n  ');
    }
    if (result.passed && !result.todo) {
      this.out.write('.');
    } else if (!result.passed && result.todo) {
      this.out.write('T');
    } else if (result.skipped) {
      this.out.write('*');
    } else {
      this.out.write('F');
    }
    this.currentLineChars += 1;
  }

  finish() {
    if (this.silent) {
      return;
    }
    this.endTime = new Date();
    this.out.write('\n\n');
    this.out.write(this.summaryDisplay());
    this.out.write('\n\n');
    this.displayErrors();
  }

  displayErrors() {
    this.results.forEach((data, idx) => {
      let result = data.result;
      let error = result.error;
      if (!error) {
        return;
      }

      printf(this.out, '%*d) [%s] %s\n', idx + 1, 3, data.launcher, result.name);

      if (error.message) {
        printf(this.out, '     %s\n', error.message);
      }

      if ('expected' in error && 'actual' in error) {
        printf(this.out, '\n' +
               '     expected: %s%O\n' +
               '       actual: %O\n', (error.negative ? 'NOT ' : ''), error.expected, error.actual);
      }

      if (error.stack) {
        printf(this.out, '\n%s', indent(error.stack, 5));
      }

      this.out.write('\n\n');
    }, this);
  }

  summaryDisplay() {
    let lines = [
      `[duration - ${this.duration()} ms]`,
      displayutils.summaryDisplay.call(this),
    ];
    if (this.hasBailed()) {
      let report = this.getBailReport();
      lines.push(`# bailed`);
      lines.push(`# ran before bail ${report.testsRanBeforeBail}`);
      lines.push(`# suppressed ${this.getSuppressed()}`);
    }
    return lines.join('\n');
  }

  duration() {
    return Math.round((this.endTime - this.startTime));
  }
};
EOF

cd /app && cat > lib/reporters/teamcity_reporter.js <<'EOF'
'use strict';

function namify(prefix, result) {
  let line = (prefix ? (prefix + ' - ') : '') +
    result.name.trim();

  return escape(line);
}

/**
 * Borrowed from https://github.com/travisjeffery/mocha-teamcity-reporter
 * Escape the given `str`.
 */

function escape(str) {
  if (!str) {
    return '';
  }
  return str
    .toString()
    .replace(/\|/g, '||')
    .replace(/\n/g, '|n')
    .replace(/\r/g, '|r')
    .replace(/\[/g, '|[')
    .replace(/\]/g, '|]')
    .replace(/\u0085/g, '|x')
    .replace(/\u2028/g, '|l')
    .replace(/\u2029/g, '|p')
    .replace(/'/g, '|\'');
}

function teamcityLine(type, options) {
  const attributes = Object.keys(options)
    .map(attributeName => `${attributeName}='${options[attributeName]}'`)
    .join(' ');

  return `##teamcity[${type} ${attributes}]\n`;
}

function runDurationAttribute(result) {
  return typeof result.runDuration === 'number' ? {duration: result.runDuration} : undefined;
}

module.exports = class TeamcityReporter {
  constructor(silent, out, config, app) {
    this.out = out || process.stdout;
    this.silent = silent;
    this.stoppedOnError = null;
    this.id = 1;
    this.total = 0;
    this.pass = 0;
    this.skipped = 0;
    this.startTime = new Date();
    this.endTime = null;
    this.app = app;
  }

  report(prefix, data) {
    if (this.hasBailed()) {
      return;
    }
    const name = namify(prefix, data);
    this.out.write(teamcityLine('testStarted', {name}));
    this._display(prefix, data);
    this.out.write(teamcityLine('testFinished', Object.assign(
      {name},
      runDurationAttribute(data)
    )));
    this.total++;
    if (data.skipped) {
      this.skipped++;
    } else if (data.passed) {
      this.pass++;
    }
  }

  bailOut() {
    if (this.silent) {
      return;
    }
    let report = this.getBailReport();
    let count = report.failuresByLauncher[report.bailLauncher] || 0;
    let reason = escape(report.bailReason);
    this.out.write(teamcityLine('message', {
      text: 'Bail out!',
      errorDetails: reason,
      status: 'ERROR'
    }));
    this.out.write(teamcityLine('buildStatisticValue', {
      key: 'bailedTests',
      value: count
    }));
    this.out.write(teamcityLine('buildStatisticValue', {
      key: 'testsBeforeBail',
      value: report.testsRanBeforeBail
    }));
    this.out.write(teamcityLine('buildStatisticValue', {
      key: 'suppressedAfterBail',
      value: this.getSuppressed()
    }));
    this.out.write(teamcityLine('buildProblem', {
      description: `Bail out! ${reason}`
    }));
  }

  hasBailed() {
    return !!(this.app && this.app.reporter && this.app.reporter.hasBailed());
  }

  getBailReport() {
    return this.app.reporter.getBailReport();
  }

  getSuppressed() {
    return this.app.reporter.suppressed;
  }

  finish() {
    if (this.silent) {
      return;
    }
    this.endTime = new Date();
    this.out.write('\n\n');
    this.out.write(teamcityLine('testSuiteFinished', {
      name: 'testem.suite',
      duration: Math.round((this.endTime - this.startTime)),
    }));
    this.out.write('\n\n');
  }

  _display(prefix, result) {
    if (this.silent) {
      return;
    }
    const name = namify(prefix, result);

    if (result.skipped) {
      this.out.write(teamcityLine('testIgnored', {
        name,
        message: 'pending',
      }));
    } else if (!result.passed) {
      const hasError = result.error;
      const attributes = {name};

      const message = (hasError && result.error.message) || '';
      const stack = (hasError && result.error.stack) || '';

      attributes.message = escape(message);
      attributes.details = escape(stack);

      if (
        hasError &&
        Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(result.error, 'expected') &&
        Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(result.error, 'actual')
      ) {
        attributes.type = 'comparisonFailure';
        attributes.expected = (result.error.negative ? 'NOT ' : '') + escape(result.error.expected);
        attributes.actual = escape(result.error.actual);
      }

      this.out.write(teamcityLine('testFailed', attributes));
    }
  }
};

module.exports.teamcityLine = teamcityLine;
EOF

'use strict';

var XmlDom = require('@xmldom/xmldom');
var indent = require('../utils/strutils').indent;

module.exports = class XUnitReporter {
  constructor(silent, out, config, app) {
    this.out = out || process.stdout;
    this.excludeStackTraces = config.get('xunit_exclude_stack');
    this.silent = silent;
    this.stoppedOnError = null;
    this.id = 1;
    this.total = 0;
    this.pass = 0;
    this.skipped = 0;
    this.todo = 0;
    this.results = [];
    this.startTime = new Date();
    this.endTime = null;
    this.app = app;
  }

  report(prefix, data) {
    if (this.hasBailed()) {
      return;
    }
    this.results.push({
      launcher: prefix,
      result: data
    });
    this.display();
    this.total++;

    if (data.skipped) {
      this.skipped++;
    } else if (data.passed && !data.todo) {
      this.pass++;
    } else if (!data.passed && data.todo) {
      this.todo++;
    }
  }

  finish() {
    if (this.silent) {
      return;
    }
    this.endTime = new Date();
    this.out.write(this.summaryDisplay());
    this.out.write('\n');
  }

  summaryDisplay() {
    var doc = new XmlDom.DOMImplementation().createDocument('', 'testsuite');

    var rootNode = doc.documentElement;
    rootNode.setAttribute('name', 'Testem Tests');
    rootNode.setAttribute('tests', `${this.total}`);
    rootNode.setAttribute('skipped', `${this.skipped}`);
    rootNode.setAttribute('todo', `${this.todo}`);
    rootNode.setAttribute('failures', `${this.failures()}`);
    rootNode.setAttribute('timestamp', new Date().toString());
    rootNode.setAttribute('time', `${this.duration() }`);

    for (var i = 0, len = this.results.length; i < len; i++) {
      var testcaseNode = this.getTestResultNode(doc, this.results[i]);
      rootNode.appendChild(testcaseNode);
    }

    if (this.hasBailed()) {
      var report = this.getBailReport();
      var count = report.failuresByLauncher[report.bailLauncher] || 0;

      rootNode.setAttribute('errors', `${count}`);

      var errorNode = doc.createElement('error');
      errorNode.setAttribute('message', `Bail out! ${report.bailReason} (${count})`);
      errorNode.setAttribute('type', 'bail');
      rootNode.appendChild(errorNode);

      var propertiesNode = doc.createElement('properties');
      [
        ['bailReason', report.bailReason],
        ['testsBeforeBail', report.testsRanBeforeBail],
        ['suppressedAfterBail', this.getSuppressed()]
      ].forEach(function(property) {
        var propertyNode = doc.createElement('property');
        propertyNode.setAttribute('name', property[0]);
        propertyNode.setAttribute('value', `${property[1]}`);
        propertiesNode.appendChild(propertyNode);
      });
      rootNode.appendChild(propertiesNode);

      var systemOutNode = doc.createElement('system-out');
      systemOutNode.textContent = `Bail out! ${report.bailReason} (${count})`;
      rootNode.appendChild(systemOutNode);
    }

    return doc.documentElement.toString();
  }

  hasBailed() {
    return !!(this.app && this.app.reporter && this.app.reporter.hasBailed());
  }

  getBailReport() {
    return this.app.reporter.getBailReport();
  }

  getSuppressed() {
    return this.app.reporter.suppressed;
  }

  display() {
    // As the output is XML, the XUnitReporter can only write its results after all
    // tests have finished.
    return;
  }

  getTestResultNode(document, result) {
    var launcher = result.launcher;
    result = result.result;

    var resultNode = document.createElement('testcase');
    resultNode.setAttribute('classname', launcher);
    resultNode.setAttribute('name', result.name);
    resultNode.setAttribute('time', this._durationFromMs(result.runDuration));

    var error = result.error;
    if (error) {
      var errorNode = document.createElement('error');
      var errorMessage = '';
      var errorSection = '';

      if (Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(error, 'actual') &&  Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(error, 'expected')) {
        errorMessage = 'Assertion Failed';

        errorSection += 'Expected:\n';
        errorSection += indent(`${error.expected}`);
        errorSection += '\n\n';

        errorSection += 'Result:\n';
        errorSection += indent((error.negative ? 'NOT ' : '') + error.actual);
        errorSection += '\n\n';
      }

      if (error.stack && !this.excludeStackTraces) {
        errorSection += 'Source:\n';
        errorSection += error.stack;
      }

      if (errorSection) {
        var cdata = document.createCDATASection(errorSection);
        errorNode.appendChild(cdata);
      }

      errorNode.setAttribute('message', error.message || errorMessage);
      resultNode.appendChild(errorNode);
    } else if (result.skipped) {
      var skippedNode = document.createElement('skipped');
      resultNode.appendChild(skippedNode);
    } else if (result.todo) {
      var todoNode = document.createElement('todo');
      resultNode.appendChild(todoNode);
    } else if (!result.passed) {
      var failureNode = document.createElement('failure');
      resultNode.appendChild(failureNode);
    }

    return resultNode;
  }

  failures() {
    return this.total - this.pass - this.skipped - this.todo;
  }

  duration() {
    const endTime = this.endTime ? this.endTime.getTime() : 0;
    const startTime = this.startTime.getTime();

    return this._durationFromMs(endTime - startTime);
  }

  _durationFromMs(ms) {
    if (ms)
    {
      return (ms / 1000).toFixed(3);
    } else
    {
      return 0;
    }
  }
};
EOF

Now the runners. Let me update BrowserTestRunner, ProcessTestRunner, TapProcessTestRunner.

BrowserTestRunner:
- add `this.aborted = false` in constructor
- add `abort()` method
- guard onTestResult, reportResults, onGlobalError, onTestsStart (and maybe onAllTestResults/onAfterTests? Let me guard report-producing methods only;
onAfterTests should still finish)
- `abort()` should emit abort-tests via socket and exit. Should it call finish? Let me think again. If the socket exists, after abort-tests the client
will call after-tests-complete -> onAfterTests -> finish. If no socket, exit kills process but reportResults is suppressed, so no finish. To be safe,
let me call `this.finish()` after exit in abort. But careful: for a connected browser, `finish()` will run before the client's after-tests-complete;
that's fine (guarded by finished). Actually, `finish()` for singleRun calls `this.exit()` again; process is null after first exit, so resolves. Good.

But wait, if we call finish() in abort, then the client's handleAbortTests emits after-tests-complete which reaches onAfterTests -> finish (already
finished, returns). Good.

Let me implement:
```js
abort() {
  if (this.aborted) {
    return Bluebird.resolve();
  }
  this.aborted = true;

  if (this.socket) {
    this.socket.emit('abort-tests');
  }
  return this.exit().then(() => {
    this.finish();
  });
}
```

But there's a potential double-exit issue: `exit()` sets `this.process = null` after kill. `finish()` calls `this.exit()` again if singleRun; process
is null -> resolve. Fine.

However, there's a subtlety: `exit()` when process exists returns `this.process.kill().then(() => { this.process = null; })`. In `finish()`, for
singleRun it returns `this.exit().then(...)`. Since process null, `exit()` returns resolve immediately. Good.

Now, should `abort()` call finish before or after exit? If we exit first, the process is killed, and then finish. The `finish()` resolves the onFinish
promise. That's good.

Let me also guard `onProcessExit` / `onProcessError` indirectly through reportResults guard. `onProcessExit` schedules reportResults after 1s;
reportResults guarded returns. Good.

Also, `onDisconnect` sets timers; after abort, pendingTimer may fire reportResults (guarded). Could also clear in abort but not necessary. Let me
leave.

'use strict';

const log = require('npmlog');
const BrowserTapConsumer = require('../browser_tap_consumer');
const Bluebird = require('bluebird');

const LogEntry = require('../utils/log-entry');
const toResult = require('./to-result');

module.exports = class BrowserTestRunner {
  constructor(launcher, reporter, index, singleRun, config) {
    this.launcher = launcher;
    this.reporter = reporter;
    this.running = false;
    this.config = config;
    this.index = index;
    this.launcherId = this.launcher.id;
    this.singleRun = singleRun;
    this.logs = [];
    this.currentTestContext = {};
    this.disconnectCount = 0;

    this.pendingTimer = undefined;
    this.onProcessExitTimer = undefined;
    this.exitRequested = false;
    this.aborted = false;
  }

  start(onFinish) {
    if (this.pending) {
      return;
    }

    this.finished = false;
    this.pending = true;

    return new Bluebird.Promise(resolve => {
      this.onFinish = resolve;

      if (this.socket) {
        this.socket.emit('start-tests');
      } else {
        this.launcher.start().then(browserProcess => {
          this.process = browserProcess;
          this.process.on('processExit', this.onProcessExit.bind(this));
          this.process.on('processError', this.onProcessError.bind(this));
          this.setupStartTimer();
        }).catch(err => {
          this.onProcessError(err);
        });
      }
    }).asCallback(onFinish);
  }

  stop(cb) {
    if (this.socket) {
      this.socket.emit('stop-run');
    }
    return Bluebird.resolve().asCallback(cb);
  }

  exit() {
    if (!this.process) {
      return Bluebird.resolve();
    }

    // mark that somebody (generally app.js) has requested that the
    // test runner exit. This is not an unexpected exit--this setting
    // is to tell the processExit handler that.
    this.exitRequested = true;

    log.info(`Closing browser ${this.name()}.`);
    return this.process.kill().then(() => {
      this.process = null;
    });
  }

  abort() {
    if (this.aborted) {
      return Bluebird.resolve();
    }
    this.aborted = true;

    if (this.socket) {
      this.socket.emit('abort-tests');
    }
    return this.exit().then(() => {
      this.finish();
    });
  }

  setupStartTimer() {
    this.startTimer = setTimeout(() => {
      if (this.finished || !this.pending) {
        return;
      }

      let err = new Error(
        `Browser failed to connect within ${this.launcher.config.get('browser_start_timeout')}s. testem.js not loaded?`
      );
      this.reportResults(err, 0);
    }, this.launcher.config.get('browser_start_timeout') * 1000);
  }

  clearTimeouts() {
    if (this.startTimer) {
      clearTimeout(this.startTimer);
    }
    if (this.pendingTimer) {
      clearTimeout(this.pendingTimer);
    }
  }

  tryAttach(browser, id, socket) {
    if (id !== this.launcherId) {
      return false;
    } else if (this.disconnectCount > this.launcher.config.get('browser_reconnect_limit')) {
      log.info(`socket reconnection limit has been exceeded, not attaching new socket for browser ${browser} and id ${id}`);
      return false;
    }

    log.info('tryAttach', browser, id);

    this.clearTimeouts();
    this.pending = false;
    this.socket = socket;
    this.browser = browser;
    this.logs = [];

    this.onStart.call(this);

    socket.on('tests-start', this.onTestsStart.bind(this));
    socket.on('test-result', this.onTestResult.bind(this));
    socket.on('test-metadata', this.onTestMetadata.bind(this));
    socket.on('top-level-error', this.onGlobalError.bind(this));

    socket.on('browser-console', function(/* ...args */) {
      let args = Array.prototype.slice.call(arguments);
      let type = args.shift();
      let message = args.join(' ');
      this.logs.push(
        new LogEntry(type, message)
      );
    }.bind(this));

    socket.on('disconnect', this.onDisconnect.bind(this));

    socket.on('all-test-results', this.onAllTestResults.bind(this));
    socket.on('after-tests-complete', this.onAfterTests.bind(this));

    const customBrowserSocketEvents = this.launcher.config.get('custom_browser_socket_events');

    if (customBrowserSocketEvents) {
      Object.keys(customBrowserSocketEvents).forEach((key) => {
        socket.on(key, customBrowserSocketEvents[key].bind(this));
      });
    }

    let tap = new BrowserTapConsumer(socket);
    tap.on('tests-start', this.onTestsStart.bind(this));
    tap.on('test-result', this.onTestResult.bind(this));
    tap.on('all-test-results', this.onAllTestResults.bind(this));
    tap.on('all-test-results', () => {
      this.socket.emit('tap-all-test-results');
    });

    return true;
  }

  name() {
    return this.launcher.name;
  }

  reportResults(err, code, browserProcess) {
    if (this.aborted) {
      return;
    }
    browserProcess = browserProcess || this.process;

    let result = toResult(this.launcherId, err, code, browserProcess, this.config, this.currentTestContext);
    this.reporter.report(this.launcher.name, result);
    this.finish();
  }

  onTestsStart(testData) {
    if (this.aborted) {
      return;
    }
    if (testData) {
      Object.assign(testData, {
        launcherId: this.launcherId,
      });

      this.currentTestContext = testData;
      this.currentTestContext.state = 'executing';
      if (this.reporter.testStarted) {
        this.reporter.testStarted(this.launcher.name, testData);
      }
    }
  }

  onTestResult(result) {
    if (this.aborted) {
      return;
    }
    let errItems = (result.items || [])
      .filter(item => !item.passed);

    let error = errItems[0];

    if (!error && result.todo && result.passed) {
      error = { message: 'expected todo to not pass' };
    }

    this.reporter.report(this.browser, {
      passed: !result.failed && !result.skipped,
      name: result.name,
      skipped: result.skipped,
      todo: result.todo,
      runDuration: result.runDuration,
      logs: this.logs,
      error: error,
      launcherId: this.launcherId,
      failed: result.failed,
      pending: result.pending,
      items: result.items,
      originalResultObj: result
    });
    this.logs = [];
    this.currentTestContext.state = 'complete';
  }

  onTestMetadata(tag, metadata) {
    if (this.aborted) {
      return;
    }
    this.reporter.reportMetadata(tag, metadata);
  }

  onStart() {
    this.reporter.onStart(this.browser, {
      launcherId: this.launcherId
    });
  }

  onEnd() {
    this.reporter.onEnd(this.browser, {
      launcherId: this.launcherId
    });
  }

  onAllTestResults() {
    if (this.aborted) {
      return;
    }
    log.info(`Browser ${this.name()} finished all tests.`, this.singleRun);
    this.onEnd();
  }

  onAfterTests() {
    this.finish();
  }

  onGlobalError(msg, url, line) {
    if (this.aborted) {
      return;
    }
    let message = `${msg} at ${url}, line ${line}\n`;
    this.logs.push(
      new LogEntry('error', message, this.currentTestContext)
    );

    if (this.currentTestContext && this.currentTestContext.name) {
      if (this.currentTestContext.state === 'executing') {
        message = `Global error: ${msg} at ${url}, line ${line}\n While executing test: ${this.currentTestContext.name}\n`;
      } else if (this.currentTestContext.state === 'complete') {
        message = `Global error: ${msg} at ${url}, line ${line}\n After execution of test: ${this.currentTestContext.name}\n`;
      }
    } else {
      message = `Global error: ${msg} at ${url}, line ${line}\n`;
    }

    let config = this.launcher.config;
    if (config.get('bail_on_uncaught_error')) {
      this.onTestResult.call(this, {
        failed: 1,
        name: message,
        logs: [],
        error: {}
      });
      this.onAllTestResults();
      this.finish();
    }
  }

  onDisconnect() {
    this.socket = null;
    if (this.finished) { return; }

    this.pending = true;
    this.disconnectCount += 1;
    const timeout = this.launcher.config.get('browser_disconnect_timeout');
    this.pendingTimer = setTimeout(() => {
      if (this.finished) {
        return;
      }

      this.reportResults(new Error(`Browser timeout exceeded: ${timeout}s`), 0);
    }, timeout * 1000);
  }

  onProcessExit(code) {
    let browserProcess = this.process;
    this.process = null;
    if (this.finished) { return; }

    this.onProcessExitTimer = setTimeout(() => {
      if (this.finished) {
        return;
      }

      this.reportResults(new Error(this.exitRequested
        ? 'Browser exited on request from test driver'
        : 'Browser exited unexpectedly'),
      code,
      browserProcess);
    }, 1000);
  }

  onProcessError(err) {
    let browserProcess = this.process;
    this.process = null;

    if (this.finished) { return; }

    this.reportResults(err, 0, browserProcess);
  }

  finish() {
    if (this.finished) { return; }

    clearTimeout(this.pendingTimer);
    clearTimeout(this.onProcessExitTimer);

    this.finished = true;

    if (!this.singleRun) {
      if (this.onFinish) {
        this.onFinish();
      }
      return;
    }
    return this.exit().then(() => {
      // TODO: Not sure how this can happen, but sometimes onFinish is not defined
      if (this.onFinish) {
        this.onFinish();
      }
    });
  }
};

cd /app && cat > lib/runners/process_test_runner.js <<'EOF'
'use strict';
var Bluebird = require('bluebird');

var toResult = require('./to-result');

class ProcessTestRunner {
  constructor(launcher, reporter) {
    this.launcher = launcher;
    this.reporter = reporter;
    this.launcherId = this.launcher.id;
    this.finished = false;
    this.aborted = false;
  }

  start(onFinish) {
    this.onStart();
    this.finished = false;

    return new Bluebird.Promise((resolve, reject) => {
      this.onFinish = resolve;
      this.launcher.start().then(testProcess => {
        this.process = testProcess;
        this.process.once('processExit', this.onProcessExit.bind(this));
        this.process.once('processError', this.onProcessError.bind(this));
      }).catch(reject);
    }).asCallback(onFinish);
  }

  exit() {
    if (!this.process) {
      return Bluebird.resolve();
    }

    return this.process.kill();
  }

  abort() {
    if (this.aborted) {
      return Bluebird.resolve();
    }
    this.aborted = true;
    return this.exit();
  }

  onProcessExit(code, stdout, stderr) {
    this.finish(null, code, stdout, stderr);
  }

  name() {
    return this.launcher.name;
  }

  onProcessError(err, stdout, stderr) {
    this.lastErr = err;
    this.lastStderr = stderr;
    this.finish(err, 0, stdout, stderr);
  }

  onStart() {
    this.reporter.onStart(this.launcher.name, {
      launcherId: this.launcherId
    });
  }

  onEnd() {
    this.reporter.onEnd(this.launcher.name, {
      launcherId: this.launcherId
    });
  }

  finish(err, code) {
    if (this.finished || this.aborted) {
      return;
    }
    this.finished = true;
    var runnerProcess = this.process;
    this.process = null;

    var result = toResult(this.launcherId, err, code, runnerProcess);
    this.reporter.report(this.launcher.name, result);
    this.onEnd();

    this.onFinish();
  }
}

module.exports = ProcessTestRunner;
EOF
cat > lib/runners/tap_process_test_runner.js <<'EOF'
'use strict';

var TapConsumer = require('../tap_consumer');
var log = require('npmlog');
var Bluebird = require('bluebird');

var toResult = require('./to-result');

class TapProcessTestRunner {
  constructor(launcher, reporter) {
    this.launcher = launcher;
    this.reporter = reporter;
    this.launcherId = this.launcher.id;
    this.finished = false;
    this.aborted = false;
    log.info(this.launcher.name);
  }

  start(onFinish) {
    this.onStart();
    this.finished = false;

    this.tapConsumer = new TapConsumer();
    this.tapConsumer.on('test-result', this.onTestResult.bind(this));
    this.tapConsumer.on('all-test-results', this.onAllTestResults.bind(this));

    return new Bluebird.Promise((resolve, reject) => {
      this.onFinish = resolve;
      this.launcher.start().then(tapProcess => {
        this.process = tapProcess;
        this.process.once('processError', this.onProcessError.bind(this));
        this.process.process.stdout.pipe(this.tapConsumer.stream);
      }).catch(reject);
    }).asCallback(onFinish);
  }

  exit() {
    if (!this.process) {
      return Bluebird.resolve();
    }

    return this.process.kill();
  }

  abort() {
    if (this.aborted) {
      return Bluebird.resolve();
    }
    this.aborted = true;
    return this.exit();
  }

  onTestResult(test) {
    if (this.aborted) {
      return;
    }
    test.launcherId = this.launcherId;
    this.reporter.report(this.launcher.name, test);
  }

  onAllTestResults() {
    setTimeout(() => { // Workaround Node 0.10 finishing stdout before receiving process error
      this.wrapUp();
    }, 100);
  }

  wrapUp() {
    if (this.finished || this.aborted) {
      return;
    }
    this.finished = true;
    this.process = null;
    this.onEnd();
    this.onFinish();
  }

  name() {
    return this.launcher.name;
  }

  onProcessError(err) {
    if (this.aborted) {
      return;
    }
    var result = toResult(this.launcherId, err, 0, this.process);
    this.reporter.report(this.launcher.name, result);
    this.wrapUp();
  }

  onStart() {
    this.reporter.onStart(this.launcher.name, {
      launcherId: this.launcherId
    });
  }

  onEnd() {
    this.reporter.onEnd(this.launcher.name, {
      launcherId: this.launcherId
    });
  }
}

module.exports = TapProcessTestRunner;

cd /app && cat > public/testem/mocha_adapter.js <<'EOF'
/*
mocha_adapter.js
================

Testem`s adapter for Mocha. It works by monkey-patching `Runner.prototype.emit`.

*/

/* globals mocha, emit, Mocha, Testem */
/* globals module */
/* exported mochaAdapter */
'use strict';

function mochaAdapter() {

  var results = {
    failed: 0,
    passed: 0,
    total: 0,
    pending: 0,
    tests: []
  };
  var id = 1;
  var Runner;
  var ended = false;
  var waiting = 0;
  var aborted = false;

  try {
    Runner = mocha.Runner || Mocha.Runner;
  } catch (e) {
    console.error('Testem: failed to register adapter for mocha.');
  }

  function getFullName(test) {
    var name = '';
    while (test) {
      name = test.title + ' ' + name;
      test = test.parent;
    }
    return name.replace(/^ /, '');
  }

  function emitIfNotAborted() {
    if (typeof Testem !== 'undefined' && Testem.aborted) {
      if (!aborted) {
        aborted = true;
        emit('all-test-results');
      }
      return;
    }
    emit.apply(null, arguments);
  }

  /* Store a reference to the global setTimeout function, in case it's
   * manipulated by test helpers */
  var _setTimeout = setTimeout;

  var oEmit = Runner.prototype.emit;
  Runner.prototype.emit = function(evt, test, err) {
    var name = getFullName(test);
    if (evt === 'start') {
      emitIfNotAborted('tests-start', { name: name });
    } else if (evt === 'end') {
      if (waiting === 0) {
        emitIfNotAborted('all-test-results');
      }
      ended = true;
    } else if (evt === 'test end') {
      waiting++;
      _setTimeout(function() {
        waiting--;
        if (test.state === 'passed') {
          testPass(test);
        } else if (test.pending) {
          testPending(test);
        }
        if (ended && waiting === 0) {
          emitIfNotAborted('all-test-results');
        }
      }, 0);
    } else if (evt === 'fail') {
      testFail(test, err);
    }

    oEmit.apply(this, arguments);

    function testPass(test) {
      var tst = {
        passed: 1,
        failed: 0,
        total: 1,
        pending: 0,
        id: id++,
        name: name,
        runDuration: test.duration,
        items: []
      };
      results.passed++;
      results.total++;
      results.tests.push(tst);
      emitIfNotAborted('test-result', tst);
    }

    function makeFailingTest(test, err) {
      err = err || test.err;
      var items = [{
        passed: false,
        message: err.message,
        stack: (err && err.stack) ? err.stack : undefined
      }];
      var tst = {
        passed: 0,
        failed: 1,
        total: 1,
        pending: 0,
        id: id++,
        name: name,
        runDuration: test.duration,
        items: items
      };
      return tst;
    }

    function testFail(test, err) {
      var tst = makeFailingTest(test, err);
      results.failed++;
      results.total++;
      results.tests.push(tst);
      emitIfNotAborted('test-result', tst);

    }

    function testPending() {
      var tst = {
        passed: 0,
        failed: 0,
        total: 1,
        pending: 1,
        id: id++,
        name: name,
        items: []
      };
      results.total++;
      results.tests.push(tst);
      emitIfNotAborted('test-result', tst);
    }
  };

}

// Exporting this as a module so that it can be unit tested in Node.
if (typeof module !== 'undefined') {
  module.exports = mochaAdapter;
}
EOF

cd /app && cat > public/testem/jasmine2_adapter.js <<'EOF'
/*

 jasmine2_adapter.js
 ==================

 Testem's adapter for Jasmine. It works by adding a custom reporter.

*/

/* globals emit, jasmine, Testem */
/* exported jasmine2Adapter */
'use strict';

function jasmine2Adapter() {

  var results = {
    failed: 0,
    passed: 0,
    total: 0,
    pending: 0,
    tests: []
  };
  var aborted = false;

  function emitIfNotAborted() {
    if (typeof Testem !== 'undefined' && Testem.aborted) {
      if (!aborted) {
        aborted = true;
        emit('all-test-results');
      }
      return;
    }
    emit.apply(null, arguments);
  }

  function Jasmine2AdapterReporter() {

    this.jasmineStarted = function() {
      emitIfNotAborted('tests-start');
    };

    this.specStarted = function(spec) {
      var currentTest = {
        name: spec.fullName
      };
      emitIfNotAborted('tests-start', currentTest);
    };

    this.specDone = function(spec) {

      var test = {
        passed: 0,
        failed: 0,
        total: 0,
        pending: 0,
        id: spec.id + 1,
        name: spec.fullName,
        items: []
      };

      var i, l, failedExpectations, item;

      if (spec.status === 'passed') {
        test.passed++;
        test.total++;
        results.passed++;
      } else if (spec.status === 'pending') {
        test.pending++;
        test.total++;
        results.pending++;
      } else {
        failedExpectations = spec.failedExpectations;
        for (i = 0, l = failedExpectations.length; i < l; i++) {
          item = failedExpectations[i];
          test.items.push({
            passed: item.passed,
            message: item.message,
            stack: item.stack || undefined
          });
        }
        test.failed++;
        results.failed++;
        test.total++;
      }

      results.total++;

      emitIfNotAborted('test-result', test);
    };

    this.jasmineDone = function() {
      emitIfNotAborted('all-test-results');
    };

  }

  jasmine.getEnv().addReporter(new Jasmine2AdapterReporter());
}
EOF
cat > public/testem/qunit_adapter.js <<'EOF'
/*

qunit_adapter.js
================

Testem's QUnit adapter. Works by using QUnit's hooks:

* `testStart`
* `testDone`
* `moduleStart`
* `moduleEnd`
* `done`
* `log`

*/

/* globals QUnit, emit, Testem */
/* exported qunitAdapter */
'use strict';

function qunitAdapter() {

  var results = {
    failed: 0,
    passed: 0,
    skipped: 0,
    todo: 0,
    total: 0,
    tests: []
  };
  var currentTest;
  var id = 1;
  var aborted = false;

  function emitIfNotAborted() {
    if (typeof Testem !== 'undefined' && Testem.aborted) {
      if (!aborted) {
        aborted = true;
        if (QUnit && QUnit.config && QUnit.config.queue) {
          QUnit.config.queue.length = 0;
        }
        emit('all-test-results');
      }
      return;
    }
    emit.apply(null, arguments);
  }

  function lineNumber(e) {
    return e.line || e.lineNumber;
  }

  function sourceFile(e) {
    return e.sourceURL || e.fileName;
  }

  function message(e) {
    var msg = (e.name && e.message) ? (e.name + ': ' + e.message) : e.toString();
    return msg;
  }

  function stacktrace(e) {
    if (e.stack) {
      return e.stack;
    }
    return undefined;
  }

  QUnit.log(function(params, e) {
    if (e) {
      currentTest.items.push({
        passed: params.result,
        line: lineNumber(e),
        file: sourceFile(e),
        stack: stacktrace(e) || params.source,
        message: message(e)
      });
    } else {
      if (params.result) {
        currentTest.items.push({
          passed: params.result,
          message: params.message
        });
      } else {
        currentTest.items.push({
          passed: params.result,
          actual: params.actual,
          expected: params.expected,
          stack: params.source,
          message: params.message,
          negative: params.negative
        });
      }

    }

  });
  QUnit.testStart(function(params) {
    currentTest = {
      id: id++,
      name: (params.module ? params.module + ': ' : '') + params.name,
      items: []
    };
    emitIfNotAborted('tests-start', currentTest);
  });
  QUnit.testDone(function(params) {
    currentTest.failed = params.failed;
    currentTest.passed = params.passed;
    currentTest.skipped = params.skipped;
    currentTest.todo = params.todo;
    currentTest.total = params.total;
    currentTest.runDuration = params.runtime;
    currentTest.testId = params.testId;

    results.total++;

    if (currentTest.skipped) {
      results.skipped++;
    } else if (results.failed > 0 && !results.todo) {
      results.failed++;
    } else {
      results.passed++;
    }

    results.tests.push(currentTest);

    emitIfNotAborted('test-result', currentTest);
  });
  QUnit.done(function(params) {
    results.runDuration = params.runtime;
    emitIfNotAborted('all-test-results');
  });

}
EOF

/* globals io */
/* globals module */
'use strict';

var socket;
var connectStatus = 'disconnected';

function syncConnectStatus() {
  var elm = document.getElementById('__testem_ui__');
  if (elm) {
    elm.className = connectStatus;
  }
}

function serializeMessage(message) {
  // stringify for browsers (IE <= 10) that do not support sending an
  // object via postMessage
  // No need to decycle because the messages sent from iframe to parent
  // are known and will not include arbitrary objects.
  return JSON.stringify(message);
}

function deserializeMessage(message) {
  try {
    return JSON.parse(message);
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Testem: Error deserializing message:', message);
    return {};
  }
}

function sendMessageToParent(type, data) {
  var message = {type: type};
  if (data) {
    message.data = data;
  }
  message = serializeMessage(message);
  parent.postMessage(message, '*');
}

var addListener;
if (typeof window !== 'undefined') {
  addListener = window.addEventListener ?
    function(obj, evt, cb) { obj.addEventListener(evt, cb, false); } :
    function(obj, evt, cb) { obj.attachEvent('on' + evt, cb); };
  addListener(window, 'message', handleMessage);
}

var messageListeners = {};
function handleMessage(event) {
  if (event.source !== window.parent) {
    // Ignore messages not from the parent
    return;
  }
  var message = deserializeMessage(event.data);
  var type    = message.type;
  var data    = message.data;

  if (messageListeners[type]) {
    var callback   = messageListeners[type].callback;
    var listenOnce = messageListeners[type].listenOnce;

    callback(data);

    if (listenOnce) {
      messageListeners[type] = null;
    }
  }
}

function addMessageListener(type, callback, listenOnce) {
  messageListeners[type] = {callback: callback, listenOnce: listenOnce};
}

function addMessageListenerOnce(type, callback) {
  var listenOnce = true;
  addMessageListener(type, callback, listenOnce);
}

function startTests() {
  socket.disconnect();
  sendMessageToParent('reload');
}

function initUI() {
  var markup = 'TEST\u0027EM \u0027SCRIPTS!';
  var elm = document.createElement('div');
  elm.id = '__testem_ui__';
  elm.className = connectStatus;
  elm.innerHTML = markup;
  document.body.appendChild(elm);
}

function getBrowserName(userAgent) {
  var regexs = [
    /MS(?:(IE) (1?[0-9]\.[0-9]))/,
    [/Trident\/.* rv:(1?[0-9]\.[0-9])/, function(m) {
      return ['IE', m[1]].join(' ');
    }],
    [/(OPR)\/([0-9]+\.[0-9]+)/, function(m) {
      return ['Opera', m[2]].join(' ');
    }],
    /(Opera).*Version\/([0-9]+\.[0-9]+)/,
    [/(Edg)\/([0-9]+\.[0-9]+)/, function(m) {
      return ['Edge', m[2]].join(' ');
    }],
    /(Chrome)\/([0-9]+\.[0-9]+)/,
    /(Firefox)\/([0-9a-z]+\.[0-9a-z]+)/,
    /(PhantomJS)\/([0-9]+\.[0-9]+)/,
    [/(Android).*Version\/([0-9]+\.[0-9]+).*(Safari)/, function(m) {
      return [m[1], m[3], m[2]].join(' ');
    }],
    [/(iPhone).*Version\/([0-9]+\.[0-9]+).*(Safari)/, function(m) {
      return [m[1], m[3], m[2]].join(' ');
    }],
    [/(iPad).*Version\/([0-9]+\.[0-9]+).*(Safari)/, function(m) {
      return [m[1], m[3], m[2]].join(' ');
    }],
    [/Version\/([0-9]+\.[0-9]+).*(Safari)/, function(m) {
      return [m[2], m[1]].join(' ');
    }]
  ];
  var defaultPick = function(m) {
    return m.slice(1).join(' ');
  };

  for (var i = 0; i < regexs.length; i++) {
    var regex = regexs[i];
    var pick = defaultPick;
    if (regex instanceof Array) {
      pick = regex[1];
      regex = regex[0];
    }
    var match = userAgent.match(regex);
    if (match) {
      return pick(match);
    }
  }
  return userAgent;
}

function getId(callback) {
  addMessageListenerOnce('get-id', function(id) {
    callback(id);
  });
  sendMessageToParent('get-id');
}

function init() {
  getId(function(id) {
    if (id === '-1') { // No connection required
      sendMessageToParent('no-connection-required');
    } else {
      initSocket(id);

      addListener(window, 'load', initUI);
      addMessageListener('emit-message', function(item) {
        socket.emit.apply(socket, item);
      });

      sendMessageToParent('iframe-ready');
    }
  });
}

function patchEmitterForWildcard(socket) {
  var emit = Object.getPrototypeOf(socket.io).emit;

  function onevent(packet) {
    var args = packet.data || [];
    emit.call(this, '*', packet);
    return emit.apply(this, args);
  }
  socket.onevent = onevent;
}

function initSocket(id) {
  socket = io({ reconnectionDelayMax: 1000, randomizationFactor: 0 });
  patchEmitterForWildcard(socket);

  socket.emit('browser-login', getBrowserName(navigator.userAgent), id);
  socket.on('connect', function() {
    connectStatus = 'connected';
    syncConnectStatus();
  });
  socket.on('reconnect', function() {
    this.emit('browser-relogin', getBrowserName(navigator.userAgent), id);
  });
  socket.on('disconnect', function() {
    connectStatus = 'disconnected';
    syncConnectStatus();
  });
  socket.on('start-tests', startTests);
  socket.on('tap-all-test-results', function() {
    sendMessageToParent('tap-all-test-results');
  });
  socket.on('stop-run', function() {
    sendMessageToParent('stop-run');
  });
  socket.on('*', function(event) {
    if (event.data && event.data[0].indexOf('testem:') === 0) {
      var eventName = event.data[0];
      var eventData = event.data[1];
      sendMessageToParent(eventName, eventData);
    }
  });
}

// We should only call init() if it ran in browser.
if (typeof window !== 'undefined') {
  init();
}

// Exporting this as a module so that it can be unit tested in Node.
if (typeof module !== 'undefined') {
  module.exports = patchEmitterForWildcard;
}

Now, back to the client handleAbortTests design. Given the connection iframe relays server's abort-tests to the parent, the parent's handleAbortTests
should send `after-tests-complete` back through emitMessage so the iframe forwards to server, so the runner finishes. So `emitMessage` must NOT be

Let me reconsider the phrase "blocks further emitMessage". Maybe "further" means after the abort handling, i.e., subsequent calls from the
still-running test framework are blocked. So handleAbortTests should emit the two messages BEFORE setting aborted (or bypass the guard). The cleanest:

```js

  this.emitMessage('abort-tests');
  this.emitMessage('after-tests-complete');
  this.aborted = true;
  this.emit('abort-tests');
  this.emit('after-tests-complete');
},
```

due to aborted, but local handlers fire). That satisfies:
- sets public aborted property ✓
- directly emits abort-tests and after-tests-complete (via emitMessage directly, not through regular emit queue) ✓
- blocks further emitMessage (after this, emitMessage returns) ✓

'use strict';
const expect = require('/app/node_modules/chai').expect;
const sinon = require('/app/node_modules/sinon');
const PassThrough = require('stream').PassThrough;
const Config = require('/app/lib/config');
const Reporter = require('/app/lib/utils/reporter');
const App = require('/app/lib/app');
const Server = require('/app/lib/server');
const BrowserTestRunner = require('/app/lib/runners/browser_test_runner');
const ProcessTestRunner = require('/app/lib/runners/process_test_runner');
const TapProcessTestRunner = require('/app/lib/runners/tap_process_test_runner');
const TapReporter = require('/app/lib/reporters/tap_reporter');
const DotReporter = require('/app/lib/reporters/dot_reporter');
const TeamcityReporter = require('/app/lib/reporters/teamcity_reporter');
const XUnitReporter = require('/app/lib/reporters/xunit_reporter');
const Launcher = require('/app/lib/launcher');
const FakeReporter = require('/app/tests/support/fake_reporter');
const FakeSocket = require('/app/tests/support/fake_socket');

function mockApp(bail) {
  return {
    config: {
      get: function(key) {
        switch (key) {
          case 'reporter': return new FakeReporter();
          case 'bail_on_test_failure': return bail;
          case 'xunit_intermediate_output': return false;
          default: return undefined;
        }
      }
    }
  };
}

describe('bail feature', function() {
  let sandbox, stream;
  beforeEach(function() { sandbox = sinon.createSandbox(); stream = new PassThrough(); });
  afterEach(function() { sandbox.restore(); });

  describe('Reporter', function() {
    it('extends EventEmitter', function() {
      expect(Reporter.prototype instanceof require('events').EventEmitter).to.be.true;
    });

    it('bails on first failure when true', function() {
      let r = new Reporter(mockApp(true), stream);
      r.report('launcher', {name: 't1', passed: false});
      expect(r.hasBailed()).to.be.true;
      expect(r.bailReason).to.equal('t1');
      expect(r.bailLauncher).to.equal('launcher');
      expect(r.testsRanBeforeBail).to.equal(0);
      let report = r.getBailReport();
      expect(report.bailLauncher).to.equal('launcher');
      expect(report.failuresByLauncher).to.deep.equal({launcher: 1});
      expect(report.failedTests).to.deep.equal(['t1']);
    });

    it('bails on Nth failure per launcher', function() {
      let r = new Reporter(mockApp(2), stream);
      r.report('launcher', {name: 't1', passed: false});
      expect(r.hasBailed()).to.be.false;
      r.report('other', {name: 't2', passed: false});
      expect(r.hasBailed()).to.be.false;
      r.report('launcher', {name: 't3', passed: false});
      expect(r.hasBailed()).to.be.true;
      expect(r.bailReason).to.equal('t3');
      expect(r.testsRanBeforeBail).to.equal(2);
    });

    it('does not count skipped/todo as failures', function() {
      let r = new Reporter(mockApp(true), stream);
      r.report('launcher', {name: 's', skipped: true, passed: false});
      r.report('launcher', {name: 't', todo: true, passed: false});
      expect(r.hasBailed()).to.be.false;
    });

    it('gates and counts suppressed after bail', function() {
      let r = new Reporter(mockApp(true), stream);
      let sub = sandbox.stub(r.reporters[0], 'report');
      r.report('launcher', {name: 'fail', passed: false});
      r.report('launcher', {name: 'after', passed: true});
      expect(r.suppressed).to.equal(1);
      expect(sub.callCount).to.equal(1);
    });

    it('validates config and warns', function() {
      let warn = sandbox.stub(require('/app/node_modules/npmlog'), 'warn');
      let r0 = new Reporter(mockApp(0), stream);
      expect(r0.bailOnFailures).to.equal(false);
      expect(warn.calledWith('bail_on_test_failure')).to.be.true;
      warn.resetHistory();
      new Reporter(mockApp('yes'), stream);
      expect(warn.calledWith('bail_on_test_failure')).to.be.true;
      warn.resetHistory();
      let r = new Reporter(mockApp(false), stream);
      expect(r.bailOnFailures).to.equal(false);
      expect(warn.called).to.be.false;
    });

    it('resetBailState clears', function() {
      let r = new Reporter(mockApp(true), stream);
      r.report('launcher', {name: 'fail', passed: false});
      r.resetBailState();
      expect(r.hasBailed()).to.be.false;
      expect(r.bailReason).to.equal(null);
      expect(r.bailLauncher).to.equal(null);
      expect(r.testsRanBeforeBail).to.equal(0);
      expect(r.failuresByLauncher).to.deep.equal({});
      expect(r.failedTests).to.deep.equal([]);
      expect(r.suppressed).to.equal(0);
      let report = r.getBailReport();
      expect(report.bailLauncher).to.equal(null);
      expect(report.failuresByLauncher).to.deep.equal({});
      expect(report.failedTests).to.deep.equal([]);
    });
  });

  describe('TAP reporter', function() {
    it('writes bail out and summary', function() {
      let reporterObj = new Reporter(mockApp(true), stream);
      let app = { reporter: reporterObj };
      let tap = new TapReporter(false, stream, new Config('ci'), app);
      // simulate reporter reporting through aggregator
      reporterObj.reporters = [tap];
      reporterObj.report('launcher', {name: 't1', passed: false});
      reporterObj.report('launcher', {name: 't2', passed: true});
      reporterObj.finish();
      let out = stream.read().toString();
      expect(out).to.contain('Bail out! t1 (1)');
      expect(out).to.contain('# bailed');
      expect(out).to.contain('# ran before bail 0');
      expect(out).to.contain('# suppressed 1');
    });
  });

  describe('Dot reporter', function() {
    it('writes bail out and summary', function() {
      let reporterObj = new Reporter(mockApp(true), stream);
      let app = { reporter: reporterObj };
      let dot = new DotReporter(false, stream, new Config('ci'), app);
      reporterObj.reporters = [dot];
      reporterObj.report('launcher', {name: 't1', passed: false});
      reporterObj.finish();
      let out = stream.read().toString();
      expect(out).to.contain('Bail out! t1 (1)');
      expect(out).to.contain('# bailed');
      expect(out).to.contain('# ran before bail 0');
      expect(out).to.contain('# suppressed 0');
    });
  });

    it('emits bail lines', function() {
      let reporterObj = new Reporter(mockApp(true), stream);
      let app = { reporter: reporterObj };
      let tc = new TeamcityReporter(false, stream, new Config('ci'), app);
      reporterObj.reporters = [tc];
      reporterObj.report('launcher', {name: 't1', passed: false});
      let out = stream.read().toString();
      expect(out).to.contain("##teamcity[message text='Bail out!' errorDetails='t1' status='ERROR']");
      expect(out).to.contain("##teamcity[buildStatisticValue key='bailedTests' value='1']");
      expect(out).to.contain("##teamcity[buildStatisticValue key='testsBeforeBail' value='0']");
      expect(out).to.contain("##teamcity[buildStatisticValue key='suppressedAfterBail' value='0']");
      expect(out).to.contain("##teamcity[buildProblem");
    });
  });

  describe('XUnit reporter', function() {
    it('adds bail elements', function() {
      let reporterObj = new Reporter(mockApp(true), stream);
      let app = { reporter: reporterObj };
      let xu = new XUnitReporter(false, stream, new Config('ci'), app);
      reporterObj.reporters = [xu];
      reporterObj.report('launcher', {name: 't1', passed: false});
      reporterObj.finish();
      let out = stream.read().toString();
      expect(out).to.contain('errors="1"');
      expect(out).to.contain('<error');
      expect(out).to.contain('<properties>');
      expect(out).to.contain("name='bailReason' value='t1'");
      expect(out).to.contain("name='testsBeforeBail' value='0'");
      expect(out).to.contain("name='suppressedAfterBail' value='0'");
      expect(out).to.contain('<system-out>');
    });
  });

  describe('App', function() {
    it('getExitCode returns bail error', function() {
      let config = new Config('ci');
      let app = new App(config);
      app.reporter = {
        hasBailed: function() { return true; },
        getBailReport: function() {
          return { bailReason: 'boom', testsRanBeforeBail: 5 };
        }
      };
      let e = app.getExitCode();
      expect(e.message).to.contain('Bail out!');
      expect(e.message).to.contain('boom');
      expect(e.message).to.contain('5');
      expect(e.message).not.to.contain('Not all tests passed');
    });

    it('abortRunners is idempotent and resets', function() {
      let config = new Config('ci');
      let app = new App(config);
      app.reporter = { resetBailState: sandbox.stub() };
      app.server = { broadcastAbort: sandbox.stub(), resetAbort: sandbox.stub() };
      let runner = { abort: sandbox.stub().returns(Promise.resolve()) };
      app.runners = [runner];
      let p1 = app.abortRunners();
      let p2 = app.abortRunners();
      expect(p1.then).to.be.a('function');
      expect(p2.then).to.be.a('function');
      expect(app.server.broadcastAbort.callCount).to.equal(1);
      expect(runner.abort.callCount).to.equal(1);
      app.resetBailState();
      expect(app.server.resetAbort.called).to.be.true;
      expect(app.aborted).to.equal(false);
    });
  });

  describe('Server', function() {
    it('broadcastAbort is idempotent and tolerates no io', function() {
      let s = new Server(new Config('ci'));
      expect(function() { s.broadcastAbort(); }).not.to.throw();
      s.io = { emit: sandbox.stub() };
      s.resetAbort();
      s.broadcastAbort();
      s.broadcastAbort();
      expect(s.io.emit.calledWith('abort-tests')).to.be.true;
      expect(s.io.emit.callCount).to.equal(1);
    });
  });

  describe('BrowserTestRunner', function() {
    it('abort is idempotent, emits abort-tests, suppresses results', function() {
      let reporter = new FakeReporter();
      let config = new Config('ci', { reporter: reporter });
      let launcher = new Launcher('ci', { protocol: 'browser' }, config);
      let runner = new BrowserTestRunner(launcher, reporter, null, null, config);
      let socket = new FakeSocket();
      runner.tryAttach('browser', launcher.id, socket);
      let p = runner.abort();
      expect(p.then).to.be.a('function');
      runner.abort();
      expect(socket.listeners('abort-tests')).to.have.length(1); // listener side
      runner.onTestResult({ failed: 1, name: 'x' });
      expect(reporter.results.length).to.equal(0);
    });
  });

  describe('Process runners', function() {
    it('abort is idempotent and suppresses', function() {
      let reporter = new FakeReporter();
      let config = new Config('ci', { reporter: reporter });
      let launcher = new Launcher('ci', { protocol: 'process' }, config);
      let runner = new ProcessTestRunner(launcher, reporter);
      let p = runner.abort();
      expect(p.then).to.be.a('function');
      runner.abort();
      runner.onProcessExit(1);
      expect(reporter.results.length).to.equal(0);
    });
  });

  describe('Client', function() {
    it('handleAbortTests sets aborted and blocks emitMessage', function() {
      let Testem = require('/app/public/testem/testem_client');
      Testem.aborted = false;
      Testem._isIframeReady = true;
      let calls = [];
      Testem.iframe = { contentWindow: { postMessage: function() { calls.push(arguments); } } };
      let local = [];
      Testem.on('abort-tests', function() { local.push('abort-tests'); });
      Testem.on('after-tests-complete', function() { local.push('after-tests-complete'); });
      Testem.handleAbortTests();
      expect(Testem.aborted).to.equal(true);
      Testem.emitMessage('test-result', {name: 'x'});
      expect(calls.length).to.equal(2);
      expect(local).to.include('abort-tests');
      expect(local).to.include('after-tests-complete');
    });
  });
});

    testsRanBeforeBail: this.testsRanBeforeBail,

  };

I omitted `bailReason`! The spec says getBailReport returns "testsRanBeforeBail, bailLauncher null before bail and after reset, per-launcher
failuresByLauncher plain object, and failedTests name-string array" — it does NOT list bailReason. So getBailReport intentionally excludes bailReason.

The spec says reporters output "Bail out! with reason and count". The reason is `bailReason` property on Reporter, not in getBailReport. So
sub-reporters should access `this.app.reporter.bailReason`.

from getBailReport. Good.

'use strict';
const expect = require('/app/node_modules/chai').expect;
const sinon = require('/app/node_modules/sinon');
const mochaAdapter = require('/app/public/testem/mocha_adapter');
const jasmine2Adapter = require('/app/public/testem/jasmine2_adapter');
const qunitAdapter = require('/app/public/testem/qunit_adapter');

function replaceGlobals(newGlobals, originalGlobals) {
  for (let key in newGlobals) {
    originalGlobals[key] = global[key];
    global[key] = newGlobals[key];
  }
}
function restoreGlobals(originalGlobals) {
  for (let key in originalGlobals) {
    global[key] = originalGlobals[key];
  }
}

function Runner() {}
Runner.prototype.emit = function() {};

describe('adapter abort guards', function() {
  let sandbox, globals, emit;

  beforeEach(function() {
    globals = {};
    sandbox = sinon.createSandbox();
    emit = sandbox.stub();
    global.Testem = { aborted: true };
    replaceGlobals({ emit: emit }, globals);
  });
  afterEach(function() {
    delete global.Testem;
    restoreGlobals(globals);
    sandbox.restore();
  });

  it('mocha suppresses and signals all-test-results once', function() {
    replaceGlobals({ mocha: { Runner: Runner }, Mocha: { Runner: Runner }, setTimeout: function(fn){ fn(); } }, globals);
    mochaAdapter();
    let runner = new Runner();
    runner.emit('start', {title:'a'});
    runner.emit('test end', {title:'b', state:'passed'});
    runner.emit('end', {});
    runner.emit('fail', {title:'c'}, {message:'x'});
    runner.emit('test end', {title:'d', state:'failed'});
    // all-test-results should be emitted exactly once
    let all = emit.getCalls().filter(c => c.args[0] === 'all-test-results');
    expect(all.length).to.equal(1);
    // no test-result should be emitted
    expect(emit.getCalls().filter(c => c.args[0] === 'test-result').length).to.equal(0);
  });

  it('jasmine2 suppresses and signals all-test-results once', function() {
    let env = { addReporter: function() {} };
    let added;
    env.addReporter = function(r) { added = r; };
    replaceGlobals({ jasmine: { getEnv: function() { return env; } } }, globals);
    jasmine2Adapter();
    added.jasmineStarted();
    added.specDone({ status: 'failed', fullName: 'x', id: 0, failedExpectations: [] });
    added.jasmineDone();
    let all = emit.getCalls().filter(c => c.args[0] === 'all-test-results');
    expect(all.length).to.equal(1);
    expect(emit.getCalls().filter(c => c.args[0] === 'test-result').length).to.equal(0);
  });

  it('qunit suppresses, clears queue, signals all-test-results once', function() {
    let queue = [1,2,3];
    let cfg = { queue: queue };
    let Q = {
      config: cfg,
      log: function(){}, testStart: function(){}, testDone: function(){}, done: function(){}
    };
    let testDone, done;
    Q.testStart = function(fn){};
    Q.testDone = function(fn){ testDone = fn; };
    Q.done = function(fn){ done = fn; };
    replaceGlobals({ QUnit: Q }, globals);
    qunitAdapter();
    testDone({ name: 'x', failed: 1, passed: 0, skipped: 0, todo: 0, total: 1, runtime: 1, module: '', testId: 1 });
    done({ runtime: 1 });
    expect(queue.length).to.equal(0);
    let all = emit.getCalls().filter(c => c.args[0] === 'all-test-results');
    expect(all.length).to.equal(1);
    expect(emit.getCalls().filter(c => c.args[0] === 'test-result').length).to.equal(0);
  });
});

Wait, this is a real concern. In QUnit, the sequence is testStart -> (assertions) -> testDone. If abort happens between testStart and testDone:
- testStart runs before abort: currentTest set, emit test-start (not aborted yet).
- abort arrives: aborted = true.

- testStart guarded, returns early, currentTest not set.
- testDone then crashes.

Hmm. In upstream, maybe the guard inside QUnit testDone is at the very beginning? Let me reconsider. The spec says "guard at every emission point
including before and inside deferred callbacks". For QUnit, the emission points are:
- QUnit.log callback: pushes to currentTest.items (no emit)
- QUnit.testStart: emits tests-start
- QUnit.testDone: computes and emits test-result
- QUnit.done: emits all-test-results

The guard at "every emission point" means wrap the emit calls, not necessarily early-return the whole callback. But my implementation's
`emitIfNotAborted` is called only at the emit site, after the callback body already ran. So in testDone, even if aborted, it will still mutate
currentTest and then emitIfNotAborted returns early (and clears queue, emits all-test-results once). That's fine if currentTest exists.

- It does all the mutation (currentTest exists from earlier testStart)

That's fine.

All passing. Now let me think about the full flow and remaining details.

### App wiring: Reporter emits test-failure -> App.abortRunners

I added `this.reporter.on('test-failure', () => this.abortRunners());` in App.start. But there's a problem: in App.start, `this.reporter` is set
inside `Reporter.with` callback. The listener is added. Good. But when tests run in dev mode (multiple runs), after a run finishes, the reporter is
recreated each start, so the listener is fresh. Fine.

But wait — in `runTests`, the Reporter might bail and emit 'test-failure', which triggers `abortRunners`. But in dev mode, after `resetBailState` is
called (via file change?), the next run continues. Where is `resetBailState` called from? The spec says "The app exposes resetBailState". It doesn't
say it's automatically called. Maybe it's called by the dev UI or file watcher. Let me search upstream... Actually, the spec just requires the app to
expose it. I've added it. Good.

### Does App.abortRunners get called for process runners?

Yes, `this.runners` includes all runners. `Bluebird.each(this.runners, runner => runner.abort())`. Our ProcessTestRunner and TapProcessTestRunner have
abort. Good.

But what if a runner is a custom/mock runner without abort? In `createTestRunner`, all use our classes. But the dev reporter's runner? There's also
`lib/reporters/dev/runner.js` which wraps... no, that's a reporter, not a runner. Fine.

### `singleRun` concurrency and abort

When a test-failure triggers abortRunners, it broadcasts abort and aborts all runners. The `singleRun` Bluebird.map will have started runners;
aborting them causes their start promises to resolve (browser via finish, process via... wait ProcessTestRunner.abort just kills process; its start
promise won't resolve because finish is guarded by aborted). So `singleRun` promise might hang. But the App's `runTests` `.catch` handles errors. The
`currentRun` might never resolve, causing the app to hang after abort?

Let me think. When bail occurs:
1. Reporter emits 'test-failure'.
2. App.abortRunners() aborts all runners. For browser runner with socket: abort-tests sent, client finishes -> runner start promise resolves. For
process runner: abort kills process; `onProcessExit` -> `finish` guarded by aborted -> no resolve. So `singleRun` (Bluebird.map over runner.start())
waits for that process runner's start promise forever. The app's `runTests` `currentRun` never resolves -> app hangs.

Hmm. But maybe that's acceptable because `abortRunners` returns its own promise and the app can exit via `exit()` separately? Actually, the bail flow:

the realistic path and my implementation handles it.

cd /app && python3 - <<'PY'
p='lib/reporters/teamcity_reporter.js'
s=open(p).read()
s=s.replace("""  bailOut() {
    if (this.silent) {
      return;
    }
    let report = this.getBailReport();
    let count = report.failuresByLauncher[report.bailLauncher] || 0;
    let reason = escape(this.app.reporter.bailReason);
    this.out.write(teamcityLine('message', {
      text: 'Bail out!',
      errorDetails: reason,
      status: 'ERROR'
    }));
    this.out.write(teamcityLine('buildStatisticValue', {
      key: 'bailedTests',
      value: count
    }));
    this.out.write(teamcityLine('buildStatisticValue', {
      key: 'testsBeforeBail',
      value: report.testsRanBeforeBail
    }));
    this.out.write(teamcityLine('buildStatisticValue', {
      key: 'suppressedAfterBail',
      value: this.getSuppressed()
    }));
    this.out.write(teamcityLine('buildProblem', {
      description: `Bail out! ${reason}`
    }));
  }
""", """  bailOut() {
    this.emitBail();
  }

  emitBail() {
    if (this.silent || this.bailReported) {
      return;
    }
    this.bailReported = true;
    let report = this.getBailReport();
    let count = report.failuresByLauncher[report.bailLauncher] || 0;
    let reason = escape(this.app.reporter.bailReason);
    this.out.write(teamcityLine('message', {
      text: 'Bail out!',
      errorDetails: reason,
      status: 'ERROR'
    }));
    this.out.write(teamcityLine('buildStatisticValue', {
      key: 'bailedTests',
      value: count
    }));
    this.out.write(teamcityLine('buildStatisticValue', {
      key: 'testsBeforeBail',
      value: report.testsRanBeforeBail
    }));
    this.out.write(teamcityLine('buildStatisticValue', {
      key: 'suppressedAfterBail',
      value: this.getSuppressed()
    }));
    this.out.write(teamcityLine('buildProblem', {
      description: `Bail out! ${reason}`
    }));
  }
""", 1)
s=s.replace("""  finish() {
    if (this.silent) {
      return;
    }
    this.endTime = new Date();
    this.out.write('\\n\\n');
    this.out.write(teamcityLine('testSuiteFinished', {
      name: 'testem.suite',
      duration: Math.round((this.endTime - this.startTime)),
    }));
    this.out.write('\\n\\n');
  }
""", """  finish() {
    if (this.silent) {
      return;
    }
    this.endTime = new Date();
    this.emitBail();
    this.out.write('\\n\\n');
    this.out.write(teamcityLine('testSuiteFinished', {
      name: 'testem.suite',
      duration: Math.round((this.endTime - this.startTime)),
    }));
    this.out.write('\\n\\n');
  }
""", 1)
open(p,'w').write(s)

'use strict';
const expect = require('/app/node_modules/chai').expect;
const PassThrough = require('stream').PassThrough;
const Config = require('/app/lib/config');
const TapReporter = require('/app/lib/reporters/tap_reporter');
const DotReporter = require('/app/lib/reporters/dot_reporter');
const TeamcityReporter = require('/app/lib/reporters/teamcity_reporter');
const XUnitReporter = require('/app/lib/reporters/xunit_reporter');

function bailedApp() {
  return {
    reporter: {
      hasBailed: function() { return true; },
      getBailReport: function() {
        return {
          testsRanBeforeBail: 2,
          bailLauncher: 'launcher',
          failuresByLauncher: { launcher: 1 },
          failedTests: ['f1']
        };
      },
      bailReason: 'f1',
      suppressed: 3
    }
  };
}

describe('direct reporter bail output', function() {
  it('tap summary on finish', function() {
    let s = new PassThrough();
    let tap = new TapReporter(false, s, new Config('ci'), bailedApp());
    tap.finish();
    let out = s.read().toString();
    expect(out).to.contain('# bailed');
    expect(out).to.contain('# ran before bail 2');
    expect(out).to.contain('# suppressed 3');
  });
  it('dot summary on finish', function() {
    let s = new PassThrough();
    let dot = new DotReporter(false, s, new Config('ci'), bailedApp());
    dot.finish();
    let out = s.read().toString();
    expect(out).to.contain('# bailed');
    expect(out).to.contain('# ran before bail 2');
    expect(out).to.contain('# suppressed 3');
  });
  it('teamcity on finish', function() {
    let s = new PassThrough();
    let tc = new TeamcityReporter(false, s, new Config('ci'), bailedApp());
    tc.finish();
    let out = s.read().toString();
    expect(out).to.contain('Bail out!');
    expect(out).to.contain("key='bailedTests' value='1'");
    expect(out).to.contain("key='testsBeforeBail' value='2'");
    expect(out).to.contain("key='suppressedAfterBail' value='3'");
    expect(out).to.contain('buildProblem');
  });
  it('xunit on finish', function() {
    let s = new PassThrough();
    let xu = new XUnitReporter(false, s, new Config('ci'), bailedApp());
    xu.finish();
    let out = s.read().toString();
    expect(out).to.contain('errors="1"');
    expect(out).to.contain('<error');
    expect(out).to.contain('name="bailReason" value="f1"');
    expect(out).to.contain('name="testsBeforeBail" value="2"');
    expect(out).to.contain('name="suppressedAfterBail" value="3"');
    expect(out).to.contain('<system-out>');
  });
});
EOF
